## TopoStream · 代碼示例

**id**　3c927fceff1c6d819f-9

**english**　TopoStream

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 地形類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/topostream-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/topostream-class.html

**text**　# Name: TopoBoundary_Ex_02.py
# Description: Run TopoToRaster using all the supported objects.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Create all the supported Objects

myTopoPtElev = TopoPointElevation([["spots.shp", "spot_meter"], ["spots2.shp", "elev"]])
myTopoContour = TopoContour([["contours.shp", "spot_meter"]])
myTopoBoundary = TopoBoundary(["boundary.shp"])
myTopoLake = TopoLake(["lakes.shp"])
myTopoSink = TopoSink([["sink1.shp", "elevation"], ["sink2.shp", "NONE"]])
myTopoStream = TopoStream(["streams.shp"])
# Run TopoToRaster
outTopoToRaster = TopoToRaster([myTopoPtElev, myTopoContour, myTopoBoundary, myTopoLake, myTopoSink, myTopoStream])
# Save the output
outTopoToRaster.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/ttraster2")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/topostream-class.html#properties

## TimeSpecialDays · 代碼示例

**id**　3d4c29b406a08c94b6-7

**english**　TimeSpecialDays

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 時間類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/timespecialdays-class.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/timespecialdays-class.html

**text**　# Name: TimeSpecialDays_Ex_02.py
# Description: Run AreaSolarRadiation using the TimeSpecialDays object
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "solar_dem"

# Create TimeSpecialDays Object
myTimeSpecialDay = TimeSpecialDays()

# Run AreaSolarRadiation
outAreaSolar = AreaSolarRadiation(inRaster, "", 200, myTimeSpecialDay, 14,
                                  0.5, "NOINTERVAL", 1, "FROM_DEM", 32, 8, 8,
                                  "UNIFORM_SKY", 0.3, 0.5)

# Save the output
outAreaSolar.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/areasolartsd2")

**text**　輸入網格必須為 3 波段無符號 8 位影像。
要使用此函數，指定 spectral_detail 和 spatial_detail 參數或 spectral_radius 和 spatial_radius 參數。這兩個參數對關系的相反。
有關此函數工作原理的詳細信息，請參閱 Mean Shift 影像分割 網格函數。
網格對象所引用的網格資料集是臨時性的。 要將其設置為永久，可以調用網格對象的 save 方法。

## SegMeanShift · 代碼示例

**id**　3d77cb0a929624cdd1-14

**english**　SegMeanShift

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 分類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/segmeanshift.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/segmeanshift.html

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set the analysis environments
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/arcpyExamples/data"

# Set the local variables
raster = "Multispectral_Landsat.tif"
spectral_detail = 15.5
spatial_detail = 15
spectral_radius = None
spatial_radius = None
min_num_pixels_per_segment = 20

# Apply SegMeanShift function
out_segmented_raster = SegMeanShift(raster, spectral_detail, spatial_detail,
                                    spectral_radius, spatial_radius,
                                    min_num_pixels_per_segment)

# Save the output
out_segmented_raster.save(
    "C:/arcpyExamples/outputs/Multispectral_Landsat_seg.crf")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/segmeanshift.html#code-sample

## 關系規則屬性 · 代碼示例

**id**　3e1ad6cf9bb5e35de6-12

**english**　Relationship Rules properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 關系類屬性

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/relationshiprules-properties.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/relationshiprules-properties.html

**text**　import arcpy
desc = arcpy.Describe(r'D:\Riverside.gdb\Electric\PolesToTransformers')
for rule in desc.relationshipRules:
    print("Rule id: {}".format(rule.ruleID))
    print("Destination Class ID: {}".format(rule.destinationClassID))
    print("Destination Subtype Code: {}".format(rule.destinationSubtypeCode))
    print("Origin Class ID: {}".format(rule.originClassID))
    print("Origin Subtype Code: {}".format(rule.originSubtypeCode))
    print("Destination Min Cardinality: {}".format(rule.destinationMinimumCardinality))
    print("Destination Max Cardinality: {}".format(rule.destinationMaximumCardinality))
    print("Origin Min Cardinality: {}".format(rule.originMinimumCardinality))
    print("Origin Max Cardinality: {}".format(rule.originMaximumCardinality))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#summary

## setTimeInclusion · 討論

**id**　3e210f5f5d78ce599d-2

**english**　MapTime

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html

**text**　MapTime 類允許您使用許多可用的時間設置來過濾地圖中的圖徵，這些時間設置對應于 時間 選項卡上的控件和 ArcGIS Pro 的布局中已激活的地圖框上的 時間滑塊 。 更改地圖時間屬性僅適用于與 布局 上的 MapFrame 關聯的地圖，當前不適用于地圖視圖。
currentStartTime 和 currentEndTime 屬性用于指定時間跨度。 timeInclusion 屬性可控制時間跨度中是否包含圖徵。 該屬性使用 setTimeInclusion 方法進行設置。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#discussion

## setTimeInclusion · 提示：

**id**　3e210f5f5d78ce599d-3

**english**　MapTime

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html

**text**　currentStartTime 屬性不能大于 currentEndTime 屬性。 相反， currentEndTime 屬性不能小于 currentStartTime 屬性。 如果您要更改為新的未來時間跨度，則最好在設置 currentStartTime 之前設置 currentEndTime 。
timeStep 方法可用于計算新的 currentStartTime 和 currentEndTime 值。 若要創建時間地圖序列，請使用 timeStep 方法來迭代時間值。 有關代碼示例，請參閱下方的示例 3。
可以在地圖框的地圖上新添加的圖層上啟用時間，從而使您可以執行時間分析、創建時態地圖冊等。 有關代碼示例，請參閱下面的示例 2 和 3。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#discussion

## setTimeInclusion · 代碼示例

**id**　3e210f5f5d78ce599d-20

**english**　MapTime

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html

**text**　以下腳本為地圖框設置了初始開始時間和結束時間，然后在一年中以一個月為時間間隔進行迭代。 每個月都會導出到 PDF 文件。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#method-return-value-timeStep

## setTimeInclusion · 代碼示例

**id**　3e210f5f5d78ce599d-21

**english**　MapTime

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#method-return-value-timeStep

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html

**text**　import arcpy
import os
path = r"C:\Projects\Time\Ships"

#Reference a project, layout, and a map frame
p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(os.path.join(path, "ships.aprx"))
lyt = p.listLayouts('ships')[0]
mf = lyt.listElements('MAPFRAME_ELEMENT', 'ShipsWithTimeMF')[0]

if not mf.time is None:
  #Get the map frame time, set the initial time window and inclusion settings
  t = mf.time
  t.currentTimeStart = datetime.datetime(1776, 1, 1, 0, 0)
  t.currentTimeEnd = datetime.datetime(1776, 2, 1, 0, 0)
  t.setTimeInclusion('INCLUDE_ONLY_START')

  for x in range(1,13):
    #Export to PDF
    outPDF = os.path.join(path, "Output", f"1776-{x}.pdf")
    lyt.exportToPDF(outPDF)

    #Change the time window to the next 1 month interval
    t.currentTimeStart = t.timeStep(t.currentTimeStart, 1, "months")
    t.currentTimeEnd = t.timeStep(t.currentTimeEnd, 1, "months")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#method-return-value-timeStep

## setTimeInclusion · 代碼示例

**id**　3e210f5f5d78ce599d-23

**english**　MapTime

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#method-return-value-timeStep

**text**　import arcpy
import os
path = r"C:\Projects\Time\Ships"

#Reference a project, layout, and a map frame
p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(os.path.join(path, "ships.aprx"))
lyt = p.listLayouts('Ships')[0]
mf = lyt.listElements('MAPFRAME_ELEMENT', 'NoTimeMF')[0]

#Reference the map frame's map and add a time enabled layer from a layerfile
m = mf.map
m.addDataFromPath(os.path.join(path, "ShipPositions.lyrx"))

#Zoom to the layer
l = m.listLayers()[0]
mf.camera.setExtent(mf.getLayerExtent(l))

#Enable time on the map and set its time window
mt = mf.time
mt.isTimeEnabled = True
mt.currentTimeEnd = datetime.datetime(1900, 1, 1, 0, 0)
mt.currentTimeStart = datetime.datetime(1800, 1, 1, 0, 0)
mt.setTimeInclusion('INCLUDE_ONLY_START')

#Export the layout
lyt.exportToPDF(os.path.join(path, output, "ShipPositions_1800s.pdf"))

## setTimeInclusion · 代碼示例

**id**　3e210f5f5d78ce599d-25

**english**　MapTime

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#method-return-value-timeStep

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html

**text**　import arcpy, os

basedir = r"C:\Project\Time"

# Create the output pdf file
pdfPath = os.path.join(basedir, "output", "HistoricalBoundaries.pdf")
pdfDoc = arcpy.mp.PDFDocumentCreate(pdfPath)

# Reference project, layout, map frame and map
aprx = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(os.path.join(basedir, "Tokyo.aprx"))
layout = aprx.listLayouts('Historical Boundaries')[0]
mfTokyo = layout.listElements('MAPFRAME_ELEMENT', 'Tokyo Map Frame')[0]
mTokyo = mfTokyo.map

# Add a layer that contains time values to the map
lyrTokyo = mTokyo.addLayer(arcpy.mp.LayerFile(os.path.join(basedir, 'Tokyo_Historic_Boundaries.lyrx')), 'TOP')[0]

# Enable time on the newly added layer
lyrTokyo.enableTime(startTimeField='Year', endTimeField='YearEnd')

# Enable Map Time
mtTokyo = mfTokyo.time
mtTokyo.isTimeEnabled = True

# Access Map Time properties for use in the MapTime.timeStep() method
interval = mtTokyo.currentTimeSpan
unit = mtTokyo.currentTimeSpanUnits

# Use the MapTime.timeStep() method to get the first time span
start = lyrTokyo.time.startTime
end = mtTokyo.timeStep(start, interval, unit)

# Loop through time spans
while end < lyrTokyo.time.endTime:

    # Update the map frame with the time span
    mtTokyo.currentTimeStart = start
    mtTokyo.currentTimeEnd = end

    # Export the time span to pdf
    output_pdf_file = os.path.join(basedir, "output", f"HistoricalBoundaries_{start.year}_to_{end.year}.pdf")
    layout.exportToPDF(os.path.join(basedir, "output", output_pdf_file))

    # Appened the time step pdf to the output pdf
    pdfDoc.appendPages(output_pdf_file)

    # Use the MapTime.timeStep() method to proceed to the next time span
    start = end
    end = mtTokyo.timeStep(end, interval, unit)

# Add title and end pages to create a complete map book
pdfDoc.insertPages(os.path.join(basedir, 'Title Page.pdf'), 1)
pdfDoc.appendPages(os.path.join(basedir, 'End Page.pdf'))

# Save the output pdf
pdfDoc.saveAndClose()

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/maptime-class.html#method-return-value-timeStep

**text**　定義一個邏輯增長變換函數，該函數是根據最大值、最小值和 y 截距百分比這三個形狀控制參數，以及確定函數應用范圍的閾值上限和下限確定的。
了解有關各參數對此變換函數作用的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/tflogisticgrowth-class.html#summary

## TfLogisticGrowth · 討論

**id**　3eb807a3d330ba572f-2

**english**　TfLogisticGrowth

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 變換函數類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/tflogisticgrowth-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/tflogisticgrowth-class.html

**text**　使用 TfLogisticGrowth 對象的工具為 按函數重設等級 。
函數值的范圍為從 0 到 100，此范圍隨后將轉換為評估等級。
如果設置的 minimum 參數大于 lowerThreshold 值，或者設置的 maximum 參數小于 upperThreshold 值，則函數曲線將以較快速率增長。如果設置的 minimum 參數小于 lowerThreshold 值，或者設置的 maximum 參數大于 upperThreshold 值，則函數曲線將以較慢速率增長。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/tflogisticgrowth-class.html#discussion

- 確定邏輯增長曲線的增長部分的取值范圍。 yinterceptPercent 越小，曲線增長部分的輸入值范圍就越小，但輸入值對應的優先級會加速提升。 yinterceptPercent 越小，邏輯增長曲線越明顯。 yInterceptPercent 必須介于 0 和 50 之間。 (默認值為 1.0)

**english**　TfLogisticGrowth

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 變換函數類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/tflogisticgrowth-class.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/tflogisticgrowth-class.html

**parameter**　valueAboveThreshold

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- valueAboveThreshold （可選）
- 此用戶定義的值用于分配輸入值大于 upperThreshold 的輸出像元位置。 valueAboveThreshold 的值可以為浮點數、整數或 NoData 。在工具對話框內， NoData 左右不使用引號；但在編寫腳本時需要使用引號，即 "NoData" 。 (默認值為 None)
- Variant
